  2406	    83	/** The supported platform enum, validated wherever a platform id is intake. */
  2407	    84	export const PLATFORMS = ['claude-code', 'codex', 'cursor'];
  2408	    85	
  2409	    86	/**
  2410	    87	 * Validate a platform id against the enum (surface-audit fold): a typo'd
  2411	    88	 * platform used to silently seed persistent state whose bogus origin then
  2412	    89	 * rejected the REAL platform's checkpoints as foreign.
  2413	    90	 * @param {string | boolean | undefined} value
  2414	    91	 * @returns {string | null} an error message, or null when valid/absent
  2415	    92	 */
  2416	    93	export function platformError(value) {
  2417	    94	  if (value === undefined) return null;
  2418	    95	  if (typeof value === 'string' && PLATFORMS.includes(value)) return null;
  2419	    96	  return `platform must be one of ${PLATFORMS.join('|')} (got ${JSON.stringify(value)})`;
  2420	    97	}
  2421	    98	
  2422	    99	/**
  2423	   100	 * Strict flag parser (surface-audit fold; docs/design/core.md §tooling always
  2424	   101	 * specified strict parsing). spec maps each flag name to 'string' | 'boolean';
  2425	   102	 * `json` and `root` are implied common flags. Errors — unknown flag, stray
  2426	   103	 * positional, missing string-flag value — return {error} so the command exits
  2427	   104	 * 2 instead of silently losing intent: the lenient parser let a stray token
  2428	   105	 * flip SAFETY flags off (checkpoint/doctor --strict, remap --native-only,
  2429	   106	 * receive --print-prompt all read fail-dangerous exit 0).
  2430	   107	 * @param {string[]} args
  2431	   108	 * @param {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} spec
  2432	   109	 * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, error?: string}}
  2433	   110	 */
  2434	   111	export function parseFlagsStrict(args, spec) {
  2435	   112	  /** @type {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} */
  2436	   113	  const full = { json: 'boolean', root: 'string', ...spec };
  2437	   114	  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
  2438	   115	  const flags = {};
  2439	   116	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  2440	   117	    const a = args[i];
  2441	   118	    if (!a.startsWith('--')) return { flags, error: `unexpected argument '${a}' — flags are --name [value]` };
  2442	   119	    const key = a.slice(2);
  2443	   120	    const kind = full[key];
  2444	   121	    if (kind === undefined) return { flags, error: `unknown flag --${key}` };
  2445	   122	    if (kind === 'boolean') {
  2446	   123	      // A following token that is not a flag would have been this flag's
  2447	   124	      // silent "value" under the lenient parser; reject it loudly.
  2448	   125	      const next = args[i + 1];
  2449	   126	      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
  2450	   127	        return { flags, error: `unexpected token '${next}' after --${key} (a bare flag takes no value)` };
  2451	   128	      }
  2452	   129	      flags[key] = true;
  2453	   130	      continue;
  2454	   131	    }
  2455	   132	    const next = args[i + 1];
  2456	   133	    // A string flag consumes the next token unless it is itself a KNOWN flag
  2457	   134	    // (so `--commit --origin x` errors instead of eating '--origin').
  2458	   135	    if (next === undefined || (next.startsWith('--') && full[next.slice(2)] !== undefined)) {
  2459	   136	      return { flags, error: `--${key} requires a value` };
  2460	   137	    }
  2461	   138	    flags[key] = next;
  2462	   139	    i += 1;
  2463	   140	  }
  2464	   141	  return { flags };
  2465	   142	}
  2466	   143	
  2467	   144	/**
  2468	   145	 * Minimal flag parser: `--key value` pairs (value = next token not starting
  2469	   146	 * with --), bare `--key` booleans, positionals collected in order.
  2470	   147	 * @param {string[]} args
  2471	   148	 * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, positionals: string[]}}
  2472	   149	 */
  2473	   150	export function parseFlags(args) {
  2474	   151	  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
  2475	   152	  const flags = {};
  2476	   153	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2477	   154	  const positionals = [];
  2478	   155	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  2479	   156	    const a = args[i];
  2480	   157	    if (a.startsWith('--')) {
  2481	   158	      const key = a.slice(2);
  2482	   159	      const next = args[i + 1];
  2483	   160	      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
  2484	   161	        flags[key] = next;
  2485	   162	        i += 1;
  2486	   163	      } else {
  2487	   164	        flags[key] = true;
  2488	   165	      }
  2489	   166	    } else {
  2490	   167	      positionals.push(a);
  2491	   168	    }
  2492	   169	  }
  2493	   170	  return { flags, positionals };
  2494	   171	}

  2657	    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
  2658	    42	
  2659	    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  2660	    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  2661	    45	
  2662	    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
  2663	    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
  2664	    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  2665	    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
  2666	    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
  2667	    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
  2668	    52	        return reject(e);
  2669	    53	      }
  2670	    54	      const r = execResults[key];
  2671	    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
  2672	    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
  2673	    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
  2674	    58	      if (code !== 0) {
  2675	    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
  2676	    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
  2677	    61	        e.code = code;
  2678	    62	        e.stdout = out;
  2679	    63	        e.stderr = err;
  2680	    64	        return reject(e);
  2681	    65	      }
  2682	    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
  2683	    67	    });
  2684	    68	  };
  2685	    69	
  2686	    70	  return {
  2687	    71	    cwd: '/repo',
  2688	    72	    env,
  2689	    73	    stdin,
  2690	    74	    stdout,
  2691	    75	    stderr,
  2692	    76	    fs,
  2693	    77	    execFile,
  2694	    78	    now: () => currentNow,
  2695	    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
  2696	    80	    execPath,
  2697	    81	    host,
  2698	    82	    pid,
  2699	    83	    startTime,
  2700	    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,

  2714	     1	// In-memory fs fake for unit tests. Synchronous subset only.
  2715	     2	// Atomicity contract: renameSync moves content in a single step, so a target
  2716	     3	// path is never observed carrying partial content. Every mutation appends a
  2717	     4	// full snapshot to `fs.__history`, letting tests enumerate intermediate states
  2718	     5	// and prove the tmp+rename discipline (target's first appearance is a rename).
  2719	     6	
  2720	     7	function fsError(code, syscall, path) {
  2721	     8	  const e = new Error(`${code}: ${syscall} '${path}'`);
  2722	     9	  e.code = code;
  2723	    10	  e.syscall = syscall;
  2724	    11	  e.path = path;
  2725	    12	  return e;
  2726	    13	}
  2727	    14	
  2728	    15	function normalize(p) {
  2729	    16	  const str = String(p);
  2730	    17	  const isAbs = str.startsWith('/');
  2731	    18	  const out = [];
  2732	    19	  for (const part of str.split('/')) {
  2733	    20	    if (part === '' || part === '.') continue;
  2734	    21	    if (part === '..') {
  2735	    22	      out.pop();
  2736	    23	      continue;
  2737	    24	    }
  2738	    25	    out.push(part);
  2739	    26	  }
  2740	    27	  return (isAbs ? '/' : '') + out.join('/');
  2741	    28	}
  2742	    29	
  2743	    30	function dirname(np) {
  2744	    31	  const i = np.lastIndexOf('/');
  2745	    32	  if (i <= 0) return '/';
  2746	    33	  return np.slice(0, i);
  2747	    34	}
  2748	    35	
  2749	    36	function basename(np, parent) {
  2750	    37	  return parent === '/' ? np.slice(1) : np.slice(parent.length + 1);
  2751	    38	}
  2752	    39	
  2753	    40	export function makeMemfs(initialFiles = {}) {
  2754	    41	  const store = new Map(); // path -> string content
  2755	    42	  const dirs = new Set(['/']);
  2756	    43	  const mtimes = new Map();
  2757	    44	  const history = [];
  2758	    45	  let clock = 0;
  2759	    46	
  2760	    47	  const snapshot = () => Object.fromEntries(store);
  2761	    48	  const record = (op, paths) => history.push({ op, paths, files: snapshot() });
  2762	    49	  const touch = (np) => mtimes.set(np, ++clock);
  2763	    50	
  2764	    51	  const mkdirp = (np) => {
  2765	    52	    let cur = '';
  2766	    53	    for (const part of np.split('/').filter(Boolean)) {
  2767	    54	      cur = cur + '/' + part;
  2768	    55	      dirs.add(cur);
  2769	    56	    }
  2770	    57	  };
  2771	    58	
  2772	    59	  const ensureParent = (np, syscall) => {
  2773	    60	    const parent = dirname(np);
  2774	    61	    if (parent === '/' ) return;
  2775	    62	    if (!dirs.has(parent)) throw fsError('ENOENT', syscall, np);
  2776	    63	  };
  2777	    64	
  2778	    65	  for (const [p, content] of Object.entries(initialFiles)) {
  2779	    66	    const np = normalize(p);
  2780	    67	    mkdirp(dirname(np));
  2781	    68	    store.set(np, String(content));
  2782	    69	    touch(np);
  2783	    70	  }
  2784	    71	
  2785	    72	  const fs = {
  2786	    73	    readFileSync(p /*, enc */) {
  2787	    74	      const np = normalize(p);
  2788	    75	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'open', np);
  2789	    76	      return store.get(np);
  2790	    77	    },
  2791	    78	
  2792	    79	    writeFileSync(p, data /*, enc */) {
  2793	    80	      const np = normalize(p);
  2794	    81	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
  2795	    82	      store.set(np, String(data));
  2796	    83	      touch(np);
  2797	    84	      record('writeFileSync', [np]);
  2798	    85	    },
  2799	    86	
  2800	    87	    appendFileSync(p, data) {
  2801	    88	      const np = normalize(p);
  2802	    89	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
  2803	    90	      store.set(np, (store.get(np) || '') + String(data));
  2804	    91	      touch(np);
  2805	    92	      record('appendFileSync', [np]);
  2806	    93	    },
  2807	    94	
  2808	    95	    renameSync(from, to) {
  2809	    96	      const nf = normalize(from);
  2810	    97	      const nt = normalize(to);
  2811	    98	      if (store.has(nf)) {
  2812	    99	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
  2813	   100	        const content = store.get(nf);
  2814	   101	        store.delete(nf);
  2815	   102	        store.set(nt, content);
  2816	   103	        touch(nt);
  2817	   104	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
  2818	   105	        return;
  2819	   106	      }
  2820	   107	      // Directory rename (atomic move of the node and all descendants) — the
  2821	   108	      // lock's dead-reclaim arbitration renames the stale lock dir aside.
  2822	   109	      if (dirs.has(nf)) {
  2823	   110	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
  2824	   111	        const pre = nf + '/';
  2825	   112	        dirs.delete(nf);
  2826	   113	        dirs.add(nt);
  2827	   114	        for (const k of [...store.keys()]) {
  2828	   115	          if (k.startsWith(pre)) {
  2829	   116	            store.set(nt + k.slice(nf.length), store.get(k));
  2830	   117	            store.delete(k);
  2831	   118	          }
  2832	   119	        }
  2833	   120	        for (const d of [...dirs]) {
  2834	   121	          if (d !== nt && d.startsWith(pre)) {
  2835	   122	            dirs.add(nt + d.slice(nf.length));
  2836	   123	            dirs.delete(d);
  2837	   124	          }
  2838	   125	        }
  2839	   126	        touch(nt);
  2840	   127	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
  2841	   128	        return;
  2842	   129	      }
  2843	   130	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rename', nf);
  2844	   131	    },
  2845	   132	
  2846	   133	    mkdirSync(p, opts = {}) {
  2847	   134	      const np = normalize(p);
  2848	   135	      if (opts.recursive) {
  2849	   136	        mkdirp(np);
  2850	   137	        record('mkdirSync', [np]);
  2851	   138	        return undefined;
  2852	   139	      }
  2853	   140	      ensureParent(np, 'mkdir');
  2854	   141	      if (dirs.has(np) || store.has(np)) throw fsError('EEXIST', 'mkdir', np);
  2855	   142	      dirs.add(np);
  2856	   143	      record('mkdirSync', [np]);
  2857	   144	      return undefined;
  2858	   145	    },
  2859	   146	
  2860	   147	    existsSync(p) {
  2861	   148	      const np = normalize(p);
  2862	   149	      return store.has(np) || dirs.has(np);
  2863	   150	    },
  2864	   151	
  2865	   152	    readdirSync(p) {
  2866	   153	      const np = normalize(p);
  2867	   154	      if (!dirs.has(np)) {
  2868	   155	        if (store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOTDIR', 'scandir', np);
  2869	   156	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'scandir', np);
  2870	   157	      }
  2871	   158	      const names = new Set();
  2872	   159	      const collect = (k) => {
  2873	   160	        if (k !== np && dirname(k) === np) names.add(basename(k, np));
  2874	   161	      };
  2875	   162	      for (const k of store.keys()) collect(k);
  2876	   163	      for (const d of dirs) collect(d);
  2877	   164	      return [...names];
  2878	   165	    },
  2879	   166	
  2880	   167	    unlinkSync(p) {
  2881	   168	      const np = normalize(p);
  2882	   169	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'unlink', np);
  2883	   170	      store.delete(np);
  2884	   171	      record('unlinkSync', [np]);
  2885	   172	    },
  2886	   173	
  2887	   174	    rmSync(p, opts = {}) {
  2888	   175	      const np = normalize(p);
  2889	   176	      const isFile = store.has(np);
  2890	   177	      const isDir = dirs.has(np);
  2891	   178	      if (!isFile && !isDir) {
  2892	   179	        if (opts.force) return;
  2893	   180	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rm', np);
  2894	   181	      }
  2895	   182	      if (isFile) store.delete(np);
  2896	   183	      if (isDir) {
  2897	   184	        const pre = np + '/';
  2898	   185	        if (!opts.recursive) {
  2899	   186	          const hasChildren = [...store.keys(), ...dirs].some((k) => k !== np && k.startsWith(pre));
  2900	   187	          if (hasChildren) throw fsError('ENOTEMPTY', 'rmdir', np);
  2901	   188	          dirs.delete(np);
  2902	   189	        } else {
  2903	   190	          for (const k of [...store.keys()]) if (k === np || k.startsWith(pre)) store.delete(k);
  2904	   191	          for (const d of [...dirs]) if (d === np || d.startsWith(pre)) dirs.delete(d);
  2905	   192	        }
  2906	   193	      }
  2907	   194	      record('rmSync', [np]);
  2908	   195	    },
  2909	   196	
  2910	   197	    statSync(p) {
  2911	   198	      const np = normalize(p);
  2912	   199	      if (store.has(np)) {
  2913	   200	        const size = Buffer.byteLength(store.get(np));
  2914	   201	        return { isFile: () => true, isDirectory: () => false, isSymbolicLink: () => false, size, mtimeMs: mtimes.get(np) || 0 };
  2915	   202	      }
  2916	   203	      if (dirs.has(np)) {
  2917	   204	        return { isFile: () => false, isDirectory: () => true, isSymbolicLink: () => false, size: 0, mtimeMs: mtimes.get(np) || 0 };
  2918	   205	      }
  2919	   206	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'stat', np);
  2920	   207	    },
  2921	   208	
  2922	   209	    // The fake cannot represent symlinks, so lstat === stat and realpath is
  2923	   210	    // normalization. Symlink-refusal behavior is proven over the real fs in
  2924	   211	    // tests/integration/symlink-escape.test.mjs.
  2925	   212	    lstatSync(p) {
  2926	   213	      return fs.statSync(p);
  2927	   214	    },
  2928	   215	
  2929	   216	    realpathSync(p) {
  2930	   217	      const np = normalize(p);
  2931	   218	      if (!store.has(np) && !dirs.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'realpath', np);
  2932	   219	      return np;
  2933	   220	    },
  2934	   221	
  2935	   222	    copyFileSync(src, dest) {
  2936	   223	      const ns = normalize(src);
  2937	   224	      const nd = normalize(dest);
  2938	   225	      if (!store.has(ns)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'copyfile', ns);
  2939	   226	      ensureParent(nd, 'copyfile');
  2940	   227	      store.set(nd, store.get(ns));
  2941	   228	      touch(nd);
  2942	   229	      record('copyFileSync', [ns, nd]);
  2943	   230	    },
  2944	   231	  };

  4544	    30	//   I1. ROOT = io.cwd (same as every other command; /repo under fakeio).

  4546	    32	//       bodies and the baton.config.json content from the packaged `templates/`

  4553	    39	//       fold F4) the seeded CONTENT must SURFACE in the written files: init MUST
  4554	    40	//       read the packaged templates, not embed its own defaults — the scaffolded
  4555	    41	//       baton.config.json parses deep-equal to the config template, and each

  4564	    50	//       and exits 0.
  4565	    51	//   I4. --dry-run is PLAN-ONLY: exit 0, a human-readable plan naming the actions,
  4566	    52	//       and the ENTIRE memfs is byte-identical before/after (io.files() deep-
  4567	    53	//       equal) — ZERO writes. This is the two-phase guarantee.
  4568	    54	//   I5. IDEMPOTENT: a second apply is all-no-ops — the full memfs after run 2 is
  4569	    55	//       byte-identical to after run 1.
  4570	    56	//   I6. .gitignore COVERING NO-OP: a pre-existing line covering .handoff/ makes

  4572	    58	//   I7. MANAGED-BLOCK OUTSIDE-BYTES INVARIANCE: for a file that already has the

  4752	     2	 * Load + validate baton.config.json, parsing role chains from their
  4753	     3	 * "platform/model[@effort]" string form into structured entries.
  4754	     4	 * Never throws: returns {config, errors} with dotted-path errors.
  4755	     5	 * @param {string} cwd @param {any} io
  4756	     6	 * @returns {{config: any | null, errors: {path: string, msg: string}[]}}
  4757	     7	 */
  4758	     8	export function loadConfig(cwd, io) {
  4759	     9	  const file = `${cwd}/baton.config.json`;
  4760	    10	  /** @type {{path: string, msg: string}[]} */
  4761	    11	  const errors = [];
  4762	    12	
  4763	    13	  let raw;
  4764	    14	  try {
  4765	    15	    raw = io.fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  4766	    16	  } catch {
  4767	    17	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json not found at ${file} — run 'baton init' to scaffold it` }] };
  4768	    18	  }
  4769	    19	  let parsed;
  4770	    20	  try {
  4771	    21	    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  4772	    22	  } catch (err) {
  4773	    23	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json is not valid JSON (${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? 'parse error'})` }] };
  4774	    24	  }
  4775	    25	
  4776	    26	  if (parsed.roles === null || typeof parsed.roles !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed.roles)) {
  4777	    27	    errors.push({ path: 'roles', msg: 'roles must be an object mapping role names to chain arrays' });
  4778	    28	  }
  4779	    29	
  4780	    30	  /** @type {Record<string, any[]>} */
  4781	    31	  const roles = {};
  4782	    32	  if (errors.length === 0) {
  4783	    33	    for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(parsed.roles)) {
  4784	    34	      if (!Array.isArray(chain)) {
  4785	    35	        errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}`, msg: 'a role chain must be an array of "platform/model[@effort]" strings' });
  4786	    36	        continue;
  4787	    37	      }
  4788	    38	      /** @type {any[]} */
  4789	    39	      const entries = [];
  4790	    40	      for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
  4791	    41	        const s = chain[i];
  4792	    42	        const slash = typeof s === 'string' ? s.indexOf('/') : -1;
  4793	    43	        if (slash <= 0 || slash === s.length - 1) {
  4794	    44	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `malformed chain entry ${JSON.stringify(s)} — expected "platform/model[@effort]"` });
  4795	    45	          continue;
  4796	    46	        }
  4797	    47	        const platform = s.slice(0, slash);
  4798	    48	        const rest = s.slice(slash + 1);
  4799	    49	        const at = rest.indexOf('@');
  4800	    50	        const model = at === -1 ? rest : rest.slice(0, at);
  4801	    51	        const effort = at === -1 ? null : rest.slice(at + 1);
  4802	    52	        if (parsed.platforms && !(platform in parsed.platforms)) {
  4803	    53	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `unknown platform "${platform}" — not a key of config.platforms` });
  4804	    54	          continue;
  4805	    55	        }
  4806	    56	        entries.push({ platform, model, effort });
  4807	    57	      }
  4808	    58	      roles[role] = entries;
  4809	    59	    }
  4810	    60	  }
  4811	    61	
  4812	    62	  if (errors.length > 0) return { config: null, errors };
  4813	    63	  return { config: { ...parsed, roles }, errors };
  4814	    64	}

  5059	     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
  5060	     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
  5061	     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], avoidEntries?: Array<{platform: string, model: string}>, nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
  5062	     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
  5063	     7	 */
  5064	     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], avoidEntries = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
  5065	     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
  5066	    10	  const notes = [];
  5067	    11	
  5068	    12	  /**
  5069	    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
  5070	    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
  5071	    15	   */
  5072	    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
  5073	    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
  5074	    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
  5075	    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
  5076	    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
  5077	    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
  5078	    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
  5079	    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
  5080	    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
  5081	    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
  5082	    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
  5083	    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
  5084	    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
  5085	    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
  5086	    30	    }
  5087	    31	    return null;
  5088	    32	  }
  5089	    33	
  5090	    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
  5091	    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
  5092	    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
  5093	    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
  5094	    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
  5095	    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
  5096	    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
  5097	    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
  5098	    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
  5099	    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
  5100	    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
  5101	    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
  5102	    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
  5103	    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
  5104	    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
  5105	    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
  5106	    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
  5107	    51	  };
  5108	    52	
  5109	    53	  // Entry-level avoidance (plan §Model-level failover): a specific
  5110	    54	  // {platform, model} tuple is dead (e.g. account-tier rejected) while its
  5111	    55	  // platform stays usable. Matching is on model IDENTITY — an @effort suffix
  5112	    56	  // on either side is ignored — and requires BOTH dimensions, so the same
  5113	    57	  // model name on another platform is never caught.
  5114	    58	  /** @param {any} m */
  5115	    59	  const baseModel = (m) => (typeof m === 'string' ? m.split('@')[0] : m);
  5116	    60	  /** @param {string} platform @param {string} model */
  5117	    61	  const entryAvoided = (platform, model) =>
  5118	    62	    avoidEntries.some((a) => a?.platform === platform && baseModel(a?.model) === baseModel(model));
  5119	    63	
  5120	    64	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
  5121	    65	  const assignments = {};
  5122	    66	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
  5123	    67	    /** @type {any[]} */
  5124	    68	    const skipped = [];
  5125	    69	    /** @type {any} */
  5126	    70	    let assignment = null;
  5127	    71	
  5128	    72	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
  5129	    73	      const entry = chain[i];
  5130	    74	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
  5131	    75	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
  5132	    76	        continue;
  5133	    77	      }
  5134	    78	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
  5135	    79	      if (why) {
  5136	    80	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
  5137	    81	        continue;
  5138	    82	      }
  5139	    83	      // After skipReason so a dual match keeps the platform-level label.
  5140	    84	      if (entryAvoided(entry.platform, entry.model)) {
  5141	    85	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'avoided-entry' });
  5142	    86	        continue;
  5143	    87	      }
  5144	    88	      assignment = {
  5145	    89	        platform: entry.platform,
  5146	    90	        model: entry.model,
  5147	    91	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
  5148	    92	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
  5149	    93	        chainIndex: i,
  5150	    94	        skipped,
  5151	    95	      };
  5152	    96	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
  5153	    97	      break;
  5154	    98	    }
  5155	    99	
  5156	   100	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
  5157	   101	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
  5158	   102	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
  5159	   103	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
  5160	   104	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
  5161	   105	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
  5162	   106	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
  5163	   107	      }
  5164	   108	    }
  5165	   109	
  5166	   110	    if (!assignment) {
  5167	   111	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
  5168	   112	    }
  5169	   113	    assignments[role] = assignment;
  5170	   114	  }
  5171	   115	
  5172	   116	  return { assignments, notes };
  5173	   117	}

  5289	docs/plans/2026-07-11-baton-v1.md:56:**Bundle** (`.handoff/`): single active bundle = `bundle.json` (atomic tmp+rename snapshot, `.bak` of previous good) + `journal.ndjson` (append-only events, torn-tail tolerant) + rendered `HANDOFF.md` + `history/` (unified retention: last 10 entries **per type** — finalize freezes, takeover archives, receive archives — each = frozen snapshot plus rotated journal; receipts needed for replay/audit are always preserved within retained entries). Schema `baton/bundle@1`: origin platform/model, task goal/constraints/acceptance, plan steps with status, capped decisions log, files touched, role assignments, git branch/HEAD/dirty summary, handoff finalize block (`status: open|sealed|received` + `receive_log[]`), compaction markers.   5290	docs/plans/2026-07-11-baton-v1.md:60:**Journal rotation & crash recovery** (Gate-1 additions, iterations 1–3): on every finalize, crash-safe order = write a rotation marker → write frozen snapshot to `history/` → rename `journal.ndjson` alongside it → start a fresh journal seeded with the snapshot's `journalSeq` → clear marker. On load, a present marker deterministically rolls the rotation forward (if the snapshot landed) or back (if not); the same marker pattern governs interrupted forced takeovers and interrupted purges (purge resumes from its marker until the whole tree is clean). Retention: last 10 rotations pruned with the history. Doctor warns at journal > 5 MB; perf test: 10k-event replay < 1 s. **Filesystem support boundary: v1 supports local filesystems only** — lock and rename atomicity are not guaranteed on network/shared mounts (NFS/SMB/cloud-sync folders); doctor detects and warns, README states it.

  5542	 succeeded in 0ms:
  5543	   140	
  5544	   141	"Survives the operator's session ending" is a defined mechanism, not a hope:
  5545	   142	
  5546	   143	- `baton loop run` is FOREGROUND by default. `--detach` re-launches the
  5547	   144	  supervisor in its own session (`setsid`), output to
  5548	   145	  `.handoff/loop/supervisor.log`, and returns.
  5549	   146	- A run lock `.handoff/loop/supervisor.lock` carries {pid, process
  5550	   147	  start-time, host, runId} — the same provably-dead machinery as the
  5551	   148	  Layer-1 bundle lock. A second `loop run` against a LIVE supervisor is
  5552	   149	  refused; against a provably-dead one it recovers.
  5553	   150	- The state machine is journal-replayable: recovery replays
  5554	   151	  `journal.ndjson`, marks any in-flight child of the dead supervisor as
  5555	   152	  interrupted (its process group is gone or killed on adoption), and either
  5556	   153	  continues from the last completed step or parks with an escalation note —
  5557	   154	  never re-runs a completed gate iteration.
  5558	   155	- Acceptance adds a supervisor-death test: kill the supervisor (and its
  5559	   156	  launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
  5560	   157	  journal and continues or parks; no orphaned children survive (process
  5561	   158	  groups reaped on adoption).
  5562	   159	
  5563	   160	- **Smoke gate (staleness-proof — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 8; TDD order —
  5564	   161	  iteration 2, finding 2)**: after gate-1, the smoke slice is built as a TDD
  5565	   162	  mini-cycle (test-author writes the failing smoke assertion → test-verifier
  5566	   163	  approves it → implementer writes only enough to pass it — the repo TDD
  5567	   164	  contract applies to the slice, not just to subtasks); the supervisor then
  5568	   165	  runs `smoke.cmd`, records output, and STOPS, writing
  5569	   166	  `.handoff/loop/SMOKE-REVIEW.md` plus `smoke-approval.json` containing an
  5570	   167	  approval token = digest over {loop-state digest, smoke.cmd, output digest,
  5571	   168	  HEAD, content-sensitive git digest (existing snapshot machinery), child
  5572	   169	  assignment}. Resuming requires `baton loop run --approve-smoke <token>`;
  5573	   170	  the supervisor recomputes the digest first and REFUSES a drifted token
  5574	   171	  (anything changed since the human looked) — it parks for a fresh smoke run
  5575	   172	  instead. No full-run budget is spent before a valid approval.
  5576	   173	- **Limit failover (the baton integration — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 3)**:
  5577	   174	  an exact transaction, not a loose chain. On child death: (1) reap the
  5578	   175	  process group and freeze the transcript; (2) classify via `baton detect`;
  5579	   176	  non-limit → the retry/park path above. On a limit classification:
  5580	   177	  (3) checkpoint the loop position INTO THE BUNDLE (supervisor
  5581	   178	  `checkpoint --session loop-<runId>`) so the seal carries it and the receipt
  5582	   179	  token binds it; (4) seal via `finalize --reason-class usage-limit --root
  5583	   180	  <mainRoot>` (an already-dead/unsealed bundle takes the degraded-open path
